|\n99\t| `{local_ip}` | `<NODE0_IP>` | 占位符，用户部署时替换 |\n100\t| `{node0_ip}` | `<NODE0_IP>` | Master 节点 IP |\n101\t| `--data-parallel-size {dp_size_total}` | 计算后的总 DP 大小 | dp_size_local × node_count |\n102\t| `--data-parallel-size-local {dp_size_local}` | 单机卡数 / tp_size | 本地 DP 大小 |\n103\t| `--data-parallel-address {node0_ip}` | `<NODE0_IP>` | DP 通信地址 |\n104\t| `--data-parallel-rpc-port` | `13389` | 默认 RPC 端口 |\n105\t| `--data-parallel-start-rank` | `0` | Node 0 从 0 开始 |\n106\t| 无 `--headless` | 保持无此参数 | Master 节点无 headless |\n107\t\n108\t**Node N 修改点**：\n109\t\n110\t| 占位符 | 替换为 | 说明 |\n111\t|---|---|---|\n112\t| `/path_to_weight/{model_name}` | 用户提供的模型路径 | vllm serve 模型路径 |\n113\t| `{nic_name}` | 用户提供的网卡名称 | 网络通信网卡 |\n114\t| `{local_ip}` | `<NODE{N}_IP>` | 占位符，用户部署时替换 |\n115\t| `{node0_ip}` | `<NODE0_IP>` | 指向 Master 节点 IP |\n116\t| `--data-parallel-size {dp_size_total}` | 计算后的总 DP 大小 | 与 Node 0 相同 |\n117\t| `--data-parallel-size-local {dp_size_local}` | 单机卡数 / tp_size | 与 Node 0 相同 |\n118\t| `--data-parallel-address` | `<NODE0_IP>` | 指向 Master 节点 |\n119\t| `--data-parallel-rpc-port` | `13389` | 与 Node 0 相同 |\n120\t| `--data-parallel-start-rank` | `N × dp_size_local` | 按 dp_size_local 递增 |\n121\t| `--headless` | 添加此参数 | Worker 节点需要 headless |\n122\t\n123\t## 参数计算公式\n124\t\n125\t```text\n126\tdp_size_total = dp_size_local × node_count\n127\tdp_size_local = 单机卡数 / 输出目录命名\n\n格式：`multi_node_{model_normalized}_{N}nodes`\n\n示例：`multi_node_deepseek_v3_1_2nodes`\n\n**名称规范化**：\n- 将模型名称转换为小写\n- 将 `-` 和 `.` 替换为 `_`\n- 移除重复的下划线\n\n## 目录结构\n\n```text\n{output_dir}/\n├── `node1/run_serve.sh`\n- Node 2：`sources/run_node2.sh` → `node2/run_serve.sh`（如有）\n- Node 3：`sources/run_node3.sh` → `node3/run_serve.sh`（如有）\n\n### 步骤 3：修改各节点启动脚本\n\n**Node 0 修改点**：\n\n| 占位符 | 替换为 | Sequence\n\n**重要**：必须先启动 Node 0 (Master 节点)，再启动 Node 1 (Worker 节点)。\n\n### 1. 启动 Node 0 (Master 节点)\n\n```bash\ncd node0\n./start_container.sh\n./run_serve.sh\n```\n\n### 2. 启动 Node 1 (Worker `--data-parallel-size-local` | 2 | 本节点数据并行大小 (单机卡数/tp_size) |\n| `--data-parallel-start-rank` | Node0: 0, Node1: 2 | 数据并行起始 rank |\n| `--data-parallel-rpc-port` | 13389 | 数据并行 RPC 端口 |\n| `--headless` | Node1 有，Node0 无 | Worker 节点需要 headless 参数 |\n\n## Testing and Validation\n\n### 基本测试\n\n```bash\ncurl http://<NODE0_IP>:8004/v1/models\n```\n\n### 推理测试\n\n```bash\ncurl {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_tool-fb5a99233ee24779b6ffc7122dc1aefd","type":"tool_result","content":"# GLM-4.5/4.6/4.7\n\n## Introduction\n\nGLM-4.x series models use a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture and are foundational models specifically designed for agent applications.\n\nThe `GLM-4.5` model is first supported in `vllm-ascend:v0.10.0rc1`.\n\nThis document will show the main verification steps of the model, including supported features, feature configuration, environment preparation, single-node and multi-node deployment, accuracy and performance evaluation.\n\n## Supported Features\n\nRefer to [supported features](../../user_guide/support_matrix/supported_models.md) to get the model's supported feature matrix.\n\nRefer to [feature guide](../../user_guide/feature_guide/index.md) to get the feature's configuration.\n\n## Environment Preparation\n\n### Model Weight\n\n- `GLM-4.5`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/ZhipuAI/GLM-4.5).\n- `GLM-4.6`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/ZhipuAI/GLM-4.6).\n- `GLM-4.7`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/ZhipuAI/GLM-4.7).\n- `GLM-4.5-w8a8-with-float-mtp`(Quantized version with mtp): [Download model weight](https://modelers.cn/models/Modelers_Park/GLM-4.5-w8a8).\n- `GLM-4.6-w8a8`(Quantized version without mtp): [Download model weight](https://modelers.cn/models/Modelers_Park/GLM-4.6-w8a8). Because vllm does not support GLM4.6 mtp in October, we do not provide an mtp version. Last month, it was supported; you can use the following quantization scheme to add mtp weights to the quantized weights.\n- `GLM-4.7-w8a8-with-float-mtp`(Quantized version without mtp): [Download model weight](https://modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp).\n- `Method of Quantization`: [quantization scheme](https://ai.gitcode.com/Ascend-SACT/GLM-4.5-w8a8). You can use these methods to quantify the model.\n\nIt is recommended to download the model weight to the shared directory of multiple nodes, such as `/root/.cache/`.\n\n### Installation\n\nYou can use our official docker image to run `GLM-4.x` directly.\n\n:::::{tab-set}\n:sync-group: install\n\n::::{tab-item} A3 series\n:sync: A3\n\nStart the docker image on each node.\n\n```{code-block} /dev/davinci0 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci1 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci2 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci3 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci4 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci5 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci6 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci7 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci8 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci9 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci10 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci11 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci12 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci13 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci14 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci15 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci_manager \\\n    --device /dev/devmm_svm \\\n    --device /dev/hisi_hdc \\\n    -v /usr/local/dcmi:/usr/local/dcmi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool \\\n    -v /usr/local/bin/npu-smi:/usr/local/bin/npu-smi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/ \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info \\\n    -v /etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n    -v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n    -it $IMAGE bash\n```\n\n::::\n::::{tab-item} A2 series\n:sync: A2\n\nStart the docker image on your each node.\n\n```{code-block} bash\n   :substitutions:\n\nexport IMAGE=quay.io/ascend/vllm-ascend:|vllm_ascend_version|\ndocker run --rm \\\n    --name vllm-ascend \\\n    --shm-size=1g \\\n    --net=host \\\n    --device /dev/davinci0 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci1 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci2 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci3 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci4 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci5 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci6 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci7 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci_manager \\\n    --device /dev/devmm_svm \\\n    --device /dev/hisi_hdc \\\n    -v /usr/local/dcmi:/usr/local/dcmi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool \\\n    -v /usr/local/bin/npu-smi:/usr/local/bin/npu-smi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/ \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info \\\n    -v /etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n    -v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n    -it $IMAGE bash\n```\n\n::::\n:::::\n\nIn addition, if you don't want to use the docker image as above, you can also build all from source:\n\n- Install `vllm-ascend` from source, refer to [installation](../../installation.md).\n\nIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to set up environment on each node.\n\n## Deployment\n\n### Single-node Deployment\n\n- In low-latency scenarios, we recommend a single-machine deployment.\n- Quantized model `glm4.7_w8a8_with_float_mtp` can be deployed on 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) or 1 Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8).\n\nRun the following script to execute online inference.\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --served-model-name glm \\\n  --max-model-len 133000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n**Notice:**\nThe parameters are explained as follows:\n\n- `--async-scheduling` Asynchronous scheduling is a technique used to optimize inference efficiency. It allows non-blocking task scheduling to improve concurrency and throughput, especially when processing large-scale models.\n- `fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant` The performance of the GmmSwigluQuant fusion operator tends to degrade when the total number of NPUs is ≤ 16.\n- `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_FLASHCOMM1` Due to the FD feature of the FIA operator being invalidated by padding data introduced by this feature, we recommend disabling the `flashcomm1` feature for long-sequence (≥16k) and low-concurrency (≤8 batch size) scenarios.For long-sequence and high-concurrency scenarios, you may enable this feature to achieve improved Prefill performance.\n\n### Multi-node Deployment\n\nAlthough the former tutorial said \"Not recommended to deploy multi-node on Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8)\", but if you insist to deploy GLM-4.x model on multi-node like 2 × Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8), run the following scripts on two nodes respectively.\n\n**Node 0**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8004 \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --data-parallel-size-local 1 \\\n  --data-parallel-start-rank 0 \\\n  --data-parallel-address $local_ip \\\n  --data-parallel-rpc-port 13389 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --max-model-len 140000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --enable-auto-tool-choice \\\n  --reasoning-parser glm45 \\\n  --tool-call-parser glm47 \\\n  --served-model-name glm47 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n**Node 1**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\nnode0_ip=\"xxxx\" # same as the local_IP address in node 0\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8004 \\\n  --headless \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --data-parallel-size-local 1 \\\n  --data-parallel-start-rank 1 \\\n  --data-parallel-address $node0_ip \\\n  --data-parallel-rpc-port 13389 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --max-model-len 140000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --enable-auto-tool-choice \\\n  --reasoning-parser glm45 \\\n  --tool-call-parser glm47 \\\n  --served-model-name glm47 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n### Prefill-Decode Disaggregation\n\nWe'd like to show the deployment guide of `GLM-4.7` on multi-node environment with 2P1D for better performance.\n\nBefore you start, please\n\n1. prepare the script `launch_online_dp.py` on each node:\n\n    ```python\n    import argparse\n    import multiprocessing\n    import os\n    import subprocess\n    import sys\n\n    def parse_args():\n        parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n        parser.add_argument(\n            \"--dp-size\",\n            type=int,\n            required=True,\n            help=\"Data parallel size.\"\n        )\n        parser.add_argument(\n            \"--tp-size\",\n            type=int,\n            default=1,\n            help=\"Tensor parallel size.\"\n        )\n        parser.add_argument(\n            \"--dp-size-local\",\n            type=int,\n            default=-1,\n            help=\"Local data parallel size.\"\n        )\n        parser.add_argument(\n            \"--dp-rank-start\",\n            type=int,\n            default=0,\n            help=\"Starting rank for data parallel.\"\n        )\n        parser.add_argument(\n            \"--dp-address\",\n            type=str,\n            required=True,\n            help=\"IP address for data parallel master node.\"\n        )\n        parser.add_argument(\n            \"--dp-rpc-port\",\n            type=str,\n            default=12345,\n            help=\"Port for data parallel master node.\"\n        )\n        parser.add_argument(\n            \"--vllm-start-port\",\n            type=int,\n            default=9000,\n            help=\"Starting port for the engine.\"\n        )\n        return parser.parse_args()\n\n    args = parse_args()\n    dp_size = args.dp_size\n    tp_size = args.tp_size\n    dp_size_local = args.dp_size_local\n    if dp_size_local == -1:\n        dp_size_local = dp_size\n    dp_rank_start = args.dp_rank_start\n    dp_address = args.dp_address\n    dp_rpc_port = args.dp_rpc_port\n    vllm_start_port = args.vllm_start_port\n\n    def run_command(visible_devices, dp_rank, vllm_engine_port):\n        command = [\n            \"bash\",\n            \"./run_dp_template.sh\",\n            visible_devices,\n            str(vllm_engine_port),\n            str(dp_size),\n            str(dp_rank),\n            dp_address,\n            dp_rpc_port,\n            str(tp_size),\n        ]\n        subprocess.run(command, check=True)\n\n    if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n        template_path = \"./run_dp_template.sh\"\n        if not os.path.exists(template_path):\n            print(f\"Template file {template_path} does not exist.\")\n            sys.exit(1)\n\n        processes = []\n        num_cards = dp_size_local * tp_size\n        for i in range(dp_size_local):\n            dp_rank = dp_rank_start + i\n            vllm_engine_port = vllm_start_port + i\n            visible_devices = \",\".join(str(x) for x in range(i * tp_size, (i + 1) * tp_size))\n            process = multiprocessing.Process(target=run_command,\n                                            args=(visible_devices, dp_rank,\n                                                    vllm_engine_port))\n            processes.append(process)\n            process.start()\n\n        for process in processes:\n            process.join()\n\n    ```\n\n2. prepare the script `run_dp_template.sh` on each node.\n\n    1. Prefill node 0\n\n        ```shell\n        nic_name=\"xxxx\" # change to your own nic name\n        local_ip=\"xxxx\" # change to your own ip\n\n        export HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\n        export ASCEND_TRANSPORT_PRINT=1\n        export ACL_OP_INIT_MODE=1\n        export ASCEND_A3_ENABLE=1\n        export VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n        export ASCEND_RT_VISIBLE_DEVICES=$1\n Refer to [Using AISBench](../../developer_guide/evaluation/using_ais_bench.md) for details.\n\n2. After execution, you can get the result, here is the result of `GLM4.7` in `vllm-ascend:main` (after `vllm-ascend:0.14.0rc1`) for reference only.\n\n| dataset | version | metric | mode | vllm-api-general-chat | note |\n|----- | ----- | ----- | ----- | -----| ----- |\n| GPQA | - | accuracy | gen | 84.85 | 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) |\n| MATH500 | - | accuracy | gen | 98.8 | 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) |\n\n### Using Language Model Evaluation Harness\n\nNot tested yet.\n\n## Performance\n\n### Using AISBench\n\nRefer to [Using AISBench for performance evaluation](../../developer_guide/evaluation/using_ais_bench.md#execute-performance-evaluation) for details.\n\n### Using vLLM Benchmark\n\nRun performance evaluation of `GLM-4.x` as an example.\n\nRefer to [vllm benchmark](https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/benchmarking/) for more details.\n\nThere are three `vllm bench` subcommands:\n\n- `latency`: Benchmark the latency of a single batch of requests.\n- `serve`: Benchmark the online serving throughput.\n- `throughput`: Benchmark offline inference throughput.\n\nTake the `serve` as an example. Run the code as follows.\n\n```shell\nvllm bench serve \\\n  --backend vllm \\\n  --dataset-name prefix_repetition \\\n  --prefix-repetition-prefix-len 22400 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-suffix-len 9600 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-output-len 1024 \\\n  --num-prompts 1 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-num-prefixes 1 \\\n  --ignore-eos \\\n  --model glm \\\n  --tokenizer Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --seed 1000 \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8000 \\\n  --endpoint /v1/completions \\\n  --max-concurrency 1 \\\n  --request-rate 1\n```\n\nAfter about several minutes, you can get the performance evaluation result.\n\n## Best Practices\n\nIn this chapter, we recommend best practices for three scenarios:\n\n- Long-context: For long sequences with low concurrency (≤ 4): set `dp1 tp16`; For long sequences with high concurrency (> 4): set `dp2 tp8`\n- Low-latency: For short sequences with low latency: we recommend setting `dp2 tp8`\n- High-throughput: For short sequences with high throughput: we also recommend setting `dp2 tp8`\n\n**Notice:**\n`max-model-len` and `max-num-seqs` need to be set according to the actual usage scenario. For other settings, please refer to the **[Deployment](#deployment)** chapter.\n\n## FAQ\n\n- **Q: Startup fails with HCCL port conflicts (address already bound). What should I do?**\n\n  A: Clean up old processes and restart: `pkill -f VLLM*`.\n\n- **Q: How to handle OOM or unstable startup?**\n\n  A: Reduce `--max-num-seqs` and `--max-model-len` first. deployed on 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) or 1 Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8).\n\nRun the following script to execute online inference.\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --served-model-name glm \\\n  --max-model-len 133000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n**Notice:**\nThe parameters are explained as follows:\n\n- `--async-scheduling` Asynchronous scheduling is a technique used to optimize inference efficiency. It allows non-blocking task scheduling to improve concurrency and throughput, especially when processing large-scale models.\n- `fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant` The performance of the GmmSwigluQuant fusion operator tends to degrade when the total number of NPUs is ≤ 16.\n- `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_FLASHCOMM1` Due to the FD feature of the FIA operator being invalidated by padding data introduced by this feature, we recommend disabling the `flashcomm1` feature for long-sequence (≥16k) and low-concurrency (≤8 batch size) scenarios.For long-sequence and high-concurrency scenarios, you may enable this feature to achieve improved Prefill performance.\n\n### Multi-node Deployment\n\nAlthough the former tutorial said \"Not recommended to deploy multi-node on Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8)\", but if you insist to deploy GLM-4.x model on multi-node like 2 × Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8), run the following scripts on two nodes respectively.\n\n**Node 0**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# $local_ip \\\n  --data-parallel-rpc-port 13389 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --max-model-len 140000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --enable-auto-tool-choice \\\n  --reasoning-parser glm45 \\\n  --tool-call-parser glm47 \\\n  --served-model-name glm47 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n**Node 1**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --enable-auto-tool-choice \\\n  --reasoning-parser glm45 \\\n  --tool-call-parser glm47 \\\n  --served-model-name glm47 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n You can get the proxy program in the repository's examples: [load_balance_proxy_server_example.py](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm-ascend/blob/main/examples/disaggregated_prefill_v1/load_balance_proxy_server_example.py)\n\n```shell\nunset http_proxy\nunset https_proxy\n\npython load_balance_proxy_server_example.py \\\n    --port 8000 \\\n    --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n    --prefiller-hosts \\\n       $node_p0_ip $node_p0_ip \\\n       $node_p1_ip $node_p1_ip \\\n    --prefiller-ports \\\n       9300 9301 \\\n       9300 9301 \\\n    --decoder-hosts \\\n      $node_d0_ip \\\n      $node_d0_ip \\\n      $node_d0_ip \\\n      $node_d0_ip \\\n      $node_d1_ip \\\n      $node_d1_ip \\\n      $node_d1_ip \\\n      $node_d1_ip \\\n    --decoder-ports \\\n      9300 9301 9302 9303 \\\n      9300 9301 9302 9303\n```\n\n## Functional Verification\n\nOnce your server is started, you can query the model with input prompts:\n\n```shell\ncurl -H \"Accept: application/json\" \\\n    -H \"Content-type: application/json\" \\\n    -X POST \\\n    -d '{\n        \"model\": \"glm\", \n        \"messages\": [{ \n            \"role\": \"user\", \n            \"content\": \"The future of AI is\" \n        }], \n        \"stream\": false, \n        \"ignore_eos\": false, \n        \"temperature\": 0, \n        \"max_tokens\": 200 \n    }' http://<node0_ip>:<port>/v1/chat/completions\n```\n\n## Accuracy Evaluation\n\nHere are two accuracy evaluation methods.\n\n### Using AISBench\n\n1. Refer to [Using AISBench](../../developer_guide/evaluation/using_ais_bench.md) for details.\n\n2. After execution, you can get the result, here is the result of `GLM4.7` in `vllm-ascend:main` (after `vllm-ascend:0.14.0rc1`) for reference only.\n\n| dataset | version | metric | mode | vllm-api-general-chat | note |\n|----- | ----- | ----- | ----- | -----| ----- |\n| GPQA | - | accuracy | gen | 84.85 | 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) |\n| MATH500 | - | accuracy | gen | 98.8 | 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) |\n\n### Using Language Model Evaluation Harness\n\nNot tested yet.\n\n## Performance\n\n### Using AISBench\n\nRefer to [Using AISBench for performance evaluation](../../developer_guide/evaluation/using_ais_bench.md#execute-performance-evaluation) for details.\n\n### Using vLLM Benchmark\n\nRun performance evaluation of `GLM-4.x` as an example.\n\nRefer to [vllm benchmark](https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/benchmarking/) for more details.\n\nThere are three `vllm bench` subcommands:\n\n- `latency`: Benchmark the latency of a single batch of requests.\n- `serve`: Benchmark the online serving throughput.\n- `throughput`: Benchmark offline inference throughput.\n\nTake the `serve` as an example. Run the code as follows.\n\n```shell\nvllm bench serve \\\n  --backend vllm \\\n  --dataset-name prefix_repetition \\\n  --prefix-repetition-prefix-len 22400 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-suffix-len 9600 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-output-len 1024 \\\n  --num-prompts 1 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-num-prefixes 1 \\\n  --ignore-eos \\\n  --model glm \\\n  --tokenizer Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --seed 1000 \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8000 \\\n  --endpoint /v1/completions \\\n  --max-concurrency 1 \\\n  --request-rate 1\n```\n\nAfter about several minutes, you can get the performance evaluation result.\n\n## Best Practices\n\nIn this chapter, we recommend best practices for three scenarios:\n\n- Long-context: For long sequences with low concurrency (≤ 4): set `dp1 tp16`; For long sequences with high concurrency (> 4): set `dp2 tp8`\n- Low-latency: For short sequences with low latency: we recommend setting `dp2 tp8`\n- High-throughput: For short sequences with high throughput: we also recommend setting `dp2 tp8`\n\n**Notice:**\n`max-model-len` and `max-num-seqs` need to be set according to the actual usage scenario. For other settings, please refer to the **[Deployment](#deployment)** chapter.\n\n## FAQ\n\n- **Q: Startup fails with HCCL port conflicts (address already bound). What should I do?**\n\n  A: Clean up old processes and restart: `pkill -f VLLM*`.\n\n- **Q: How to handle OOM or unstable startup?**\n\n  A: Reduce `--max-num-seqs` and `--max-model-len` first. If needed, reduce concurrency and load-testing pressure (e.g., `max-concurrency` / `num-prompts`).","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"glm-5","id":"msg_fd4390ac-a0fa-40b2-81b5-7f270f6c2611","role":"assistant","type":"message","content":[{"name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"D:\\chenyu\\code\\Gitee\\hw_ascend_study\\code\\skills\\dev\\tests\\vllm-ascend-multi-node-zh\\scenarios\\glm4.7\\baseline\\multi_node_glm4_7_2nodes\\sources\\GLM4.md","content":"# GLM-4.5/4.6/4.7\n\n## Introduction\n\nGLM-4.x series models use a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture and are foundational models specifically designed for agent applications.\n\nThe `GLM-4.5` model is first supported in `vllm-ascend:v0.10.0rc1`.\n\nThis document will show the main verification steps of the model, including supported features, feature configuration, environment preparation, single-node and multi-node deployment, accuracy and performance evaluation.\n\n## Supported Features\n\nRefer to [supported features](../../user_guide/support_matrix/supported_models.md) to get the model's supported feature matrix.\n\nRefer to [feature guide](../../user_guide/feature_guide/index.md) to get the feature's configuration.\n\n## Environment Preparation\n\n### Model Weight\n\n- `GLM-4.5`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/ZhipuAI/GLM-4.5).\n- `GLM-4.6`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/ZhipuAI/GLM-4.6).\n- `GLM-4.7`(BF16 version): [Download model weight](https://www.modelscope.cn/models/ZhipuAI/GLM-4.7).\n- `GLM-4.5-w8a8-with-float-mtp`(Quantized version with mtp): [Download model weight](https://modelers.cn/models/Modelers_Park/GLM-4.5-w8a8).\n- `GLM-4.6-w8a8`(Quantized version without mtp): [Download model weight](https://modelers.cn/models/Modelers_Park/GLM-4.6-w8a8). Because vllm does not support GLM4.6 mtp in October, we do not provide an mtp version. Last month, it was supported; you can use the following quantization scheme to add mtp weights to the quantized weights.\n- `GLM-4.7-w8a8-with-float-mtp`(Quantized version without mtp): [Download model weight](https://modelscope.cn/models/Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp).\n- `Method of Quantization`: [quantization scheme](https://ai.gitcode.com/Ascend-SACT/GLM-4.5-w8a8). You can use these methods to quantify the model.\n\nIt is recommended to download the model weight to the shared directory of multiple nodes, such as `/root/.cache/`.\n\n### Installation\n\nYou can use our official docker image to run `GLM-4.x` directly.\n\n:::::{tab-set}\n:sync-group: install\n\n::::{tab-item} A3 series\n:sync: A3\n\nStart the docker image on each node.\n\n```{code-block} bash\n   :substitutions:\n\nexport IMAGE=quay.io/ascend/vllm-ascend:|vllm_ascend_version|-a3\ndocker run --rm \\\n    --name vllm-ascend \\\n    --shm-size=1g \\\n    --net=host \\\n    --device /dev/davinci0 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci1 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci2 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci3 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci4 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci5 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci6 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci7 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci8 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci9 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci10 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci11 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci12 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci13 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci14 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci15 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci_manager \\\n    --device /dev/devmm_svm \\\n    --device /dev/hisi_hdc \\\n    -v /usr/local/dcmi:/usr/local/dcmi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool \\\n    -v /usr/local/bin/npu-smi:/usr/local/bin/npu-smi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/ \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info \\\n    -v /etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n    -v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n    -it $IMAGE bash\n```\n\n::::\n::::{tab-item} A2 series\n:sync: A2\n\nStart the docker image on your each node.\n\n```{code-block} bash\n   :substitutions:\n\nexport IMAGE=quay.io/ascend/vllm-ascend:|vllm_ascend_version|\ndocker run --rm \\\n    --name vllm-ascend \\\n    --shm-size=1g \\\n    --net=host \\\n    --device /dev/davinci0 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci1 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci2 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci3 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci4 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci5 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci6 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci7 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci_manager \\\n    --device /dev/devmm_svm \\\n    --device /dev/hisi_hdc \\\n    -v /usr/local/dcmi:/usr/local/dcmi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool \\\n    -v /usr/local/bin/npu-smi:/usr/local/bin/npu-smi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/ \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info \\\n    -v /etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n    -v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n    -it $IMAGE bash\n```\n\n::::\n:::::\n\nIn addition, if you don't want to use the docker image as above, you can also build all from source:\n\n- Install `vllm-ascend` from source, refer to [installation](../../installation.md).\n\nIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to set up environment on each node.\n\n## Deployment\n\n### Single-node Deployment\n\n- In low-latency scenarios, we recommend a single-machine deployment.\n- Quantized model `glm4.7_w8a8_with_float_mtp` can be deployed on 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) or 1 Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8).\n\nRun the following script to execute online inference.\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --served-model-name glm \\\n  --max-model-len 133000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n**Notice:**\nThe parameters are explained as follows:\n\n- `--async-scheduling` Asynchronous scheduling is a technique used to optimize inference efficiency. It allows non-blocking task scheduling to improve concurrency and throughput, especially when processing large-scale models.\n- `fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant` The performance of the GmmSwigluQuant fusion operator tends to degrade when the total number of NPUs is ≤ 16.\n- `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_FLASHCOMM1` Due to the FD feature of the FIA operator being invalidated by padding data introduced by this feature, we recommend disabling the `flashcomm1` feature for long-sequence (≥16k) and low-concurrency (≤8 batch size) scenarios.For long-sequence and high-concurrency scenarios, you may enable this feature to achieve improved Prefill performance.\n\n### Multi-node Deployment\n\nAlthough the former tutorial said \"Not recommended to deploy multi-node on Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8)\", but if you insist to deploy GLM-4.x model on multi-node like 2 × Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8), run the following scripts on two nodes respectively.\n\n**Node 0**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8004 \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --data-parallel-size-local 1 \\\n  --data-parallel-start-rank 0 \\\n  --data-parallel-address $local_ip \\\n  --data-parallel-rpc-port 13389 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --max-model-len 140000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --enable-auto-tool-choice \\\n  --reasoning-parser glm45 \\\n  --tool-call-parser glm47 \\\n  --served-model-name glm47 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n**Node 1**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\nnode0_ip=\"xxxx\" # same as the local_IP address in node 0\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8004 \\\n  --headless \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --data-parallel-size-local 1 \\\n  --data-parallel-start-rank 1 \\\n  --data-parallel-address $node0_ip \\\n  --data-parallel-rpc-port 13389 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --max-model-len 140000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --enable-auto-tool-choice \\\n  --reasoning-parser glm45 \\\n  --tool-call-parser glm47 \\\n  --served-model-name glm47 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n## Functional Verification\n\nOnce your server is started, you can query the model with input prompts:\n\n```shell\ncurl -H \"Accept: application/json\" \\\n    -H \"Content-type: application/json\" \\\n    -X POST \\\n    -d '{\n        \"model\": \"glm\", \n        \"messages\": [{ \n            \"role\": \"user\", \n            \"content\": \"The future of AI is\" \n        }], \n        \"stream\": false, \n        \"ignore_eos\": false, \n        \"temperature\": 0, \n        \"max_tokens\": 200 \n    }' http://<node0_ip>:<port>/v1/chat/completions\n```\n\n## Accuracy Evaluation\n\nHere are two accuracy evaluation methods.\n\n### Using AISBench\n\n1. Refer to [Using AISBench](../../developer_guide/evaluation/using_ais_bench.md) for details.\n\n2. After execution, you can get the result, here is the result of `GLM4.7` in `vllm-ascend:main` (after `vllm-ascend:0.14.0rc1`) for reference only.\n\n| dataset | version | metric | mode | vllm-api-general-chat | note |\n|----- | ----- | ----- | ----- | -----| ----- |\n| GPQA | - | accuracy | gen | 84.85 | 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) |\n| MATH500 | - | accuracy | gen | 98.8 | 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) |\n\n### Using Language Model Evaluation Harness\n\nNot tested yet.\n\n## Performance\n\n### Using AISBench\n\nRefer to [Using AISBench for performance evaluation](../../developer_guide/evaluation/using_ais_bench.md#execute-performance-evaluation) for details.\n\n### Using vLLM Benchmark\n\nRun performance evaluation of `GLM-4.x` as an example.\n\nRefer to [vllm benchmark](https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/benchmarking/) for more details.\n\nThere are three `vllm bench` subcommands:\n\n- `latency`: Benchmark the latency of a single batch of requests.\n- `serve`: Benchmark the online serving throughput.\n- `throughput`: Benchmark offline inference throughput.\n\nTake the `serve` as an example. Run the code as follows.\n\n```shell\nvllm bench serve \\\n  --backend vllm \\\n  --dataset-name prefix_repetition \\\n  --prefix-repetition-prefix-len 22400 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-suffix-len 9600 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-output-len 1024 \\\n  --num-prompts 1 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-num-prefixes 1 \\\n  --ignore-eos \\\n  --model glm \\\n  --tokenizer Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --seed 1000 \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8000 \\\n  --endpoint /v1/completions \\\n  --max-concurrency 1 \\\n  --request-rate 1\n```\n\nAfter about several minutes, you can get the performance evaluation result.\n\n## Best Practices\n\nIn this chapter, we recommend best practices for three scenarios:\n\n- Long-context: For long sequences with low concurrency (≤ 4): set `dp1 tp16`; For long sequences with high concurrency (> 4): set `dp2 tp8`\n- Low-latency: For short sequences with low latency: we recommend setting `dp2 tp8`\n- High-throughput: For short sequences with high throughput: we also recommend setting `dp2 tp8`\n\n**Notice:**\n`max-model-len` and `max-num-seqs` need to be set according to the actual usage scenario. For other settings, please refer to the **[Deployment](#deployment)** chapter.\n\n## FAQ\n\n- **Q: Startup fails with HCCL port conflicts (address already bound). What should I do?**\n\n  A: Clean up old processes and restart: `pkill -f VLLM*`.\n\n- **Q: How to handle OOM or unstable startup?**\n\n  A: Reduce `--max-num-seqs` and `--max-model-len` first. If needed, reduce concurrency and load-testing pressure (e.g., `max-concurrency` / `num-prompts`)."},"id":"toolu_tool-d533074e12b0445fbb806b11bda03bed","type":"tool_use"}],"usage":{"input_tokens":66188,"output_tokens":0},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2a5e6026-86fc-499a-bb78-84b46f2d47f5","uuid":"936c5ef6-4e7d-4e80-a258-4ff690d808e1"}

/etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n    -v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n    -it $IMAGE bash\n```\n\n::::\n::::{tab-item} A2 series\n:sync: A2\n\nStart the docker image on your each node.\n\n```{code-block} bash\n   :substitutions:\n\nexport IMAGE=quay.io/ascend/vllm-ascend:|vllm_ascend_version|\ndocker run --rm \\\n    --name vllm-ascend \\\n    --shm-size=1g \\\n    --net=host \\\n    --device /dev/davinci0 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci1 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci2 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci3 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci4 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci5 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci6 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci7 \\\n    --device /dev/davinci_manager \\\n    --device /dev/devmm_svm \\\n    --device /dev/hisi_hdc \\\n    -v /usr/local/dcmi:/usr/local/dcmi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/tools/hccn_tool \\\n    -v /usr/local/bin/npu-smi:/usr/local/bin/npu-smi \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/lib64/ \\\n    -v /usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info:/usr/local/Ascend/driver/version.info \\\n    -v /etc/ascend_install.info:/etc/ascend_install.info \\\n    -v /root/.cache:/root/.cache \\\n    -it $IMAGE bash\n```\n\n::::\n:::::\n\nIn addition, if you don't want to use the docker image as above, you can also build all from source:\n\n- Install `vllm-ascend` from source, refer to [installation](../../installation.md).\n\nIf you want to deploy multi-node environment, you need to set up environment on each node.\n\n## Deployment\n\n### Single-node Deployment\n\n- In low-latency scenarios, we recommend a single-machine deployment.\n- Quantized model `glm4.7_w8a8_with_float_mtp` can be deployed on 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) or 1 Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8).\n\nRun the following script to execute online inference.\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --served-model-name glm \\\n  --max-model-len 133000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n**Notice:**\nThe parameters are explained as follows:\n\n- `--async-scheduling` Asynchronous scheduling is a technique used to optimize inference efficiency. It allows non-blocking task scheduling to improve concurrency and throughput, especially when processing large-scale models.\n- `fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant` The performance of the GmmSwigluQuant fusion operator tends to degrade when the total number of NPUs is ≤ 16.\n- `VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_FLASHCOMM1` Due to the FD feature of the FIA operator being invalidated by padding data introduced by this feature, we recommend disabling the `flashcomm1` feature for long-sequence (≥16k) and low-concurrency (≤8 batch size) scenarios.For long-sequence and high-concurrency scenarios, you may enable this feature to achieve improved Prefill performance.\n\n### Multi-node Deployment\n\nAlthough the former tutorial said \"Not recommended to deploy multi-node on Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8)\", but if you insist to deploy GLM-4.x model on multi-node like 2 × Atlas 800 A2 (64G × 8), run the following scripts on two nodes respectively.\n\n**Node 0**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8004 \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --data-parallel-size-local 1 \\\n  --data-parallel-start-rank 0 \\\n  --data-parallel-address $local_ip \\\n  --data-parallel-rpc-port 13389 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --max-model-len 140000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --enable-auto-tool-choice \\\n  --reasoning-parser glm45 \\\n  --tool-call-parser glm47 \\\n  --served-model-name glm47 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n**Node 1**\n\n```shell\n#!/bin/sh\n\n# this obtained through ifconfig\n# nic_name is the network interface name corresponding to local_ip of the current node\nnic_name=\"xxxx\"\nlocal_ip=\"xxxx\"\nnode0_ip=\"xxxx\" # same as the local_IP address in node 0\n\nexport HCCL_IF_IP=$local_ip\nexport GLOO_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport TP_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_SOCKET_IFNAME=$nic_name\nexport HCCL_BUFFSIZE=512\nexport OMP_PROC_BIND=false\nexport OMP_NUM_THREADS=1\nexport PYTORCH_NPU_ALLOC_CONF=expandable_segments:True\nexport HCCL_OP_EXPANSION_MODE=AIV\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_BALANCE_SCHEDULING=1\nexport VLLM_ASCEND_ENABLE_TOPK_OPTIMIZE=1\n\nvllm serve Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8004 \\\n  --headless \\\n  --data-parallel-size 2 \\\n  --data-parallel-size-local 1 \\\n  --data-parallel-start-rank 1 \\\n  --data-parallel-address $node0_ip \\\n  --data-parallel-rpc-port 13389 \\\n  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \\\n  --enable-expert-parallel \\\n  --seed 1024 \\\n  --max-model-len 140000 \\\n  --max-num-batched-tokens 8192 \\\n  --max-num-seqs 16 \\\n  --async-scheduling \\\n  --quantization ascend \\\n  --trust-remote-code \\\n  --gpu-memory-utilization 0.9 \\\n  --enable-auto-tool-choice \\\n  --reasoning-parser glm45 \\\n  --tool-call-parser glm47 \\\n  --served-model-name glm47 \\\n  --speculative-config '{\"num_speculative_tokens\": 3, \"method\":\"mtp\"}' \\\n  --compilation-config '{\"cudagraph_capture_sizes\": [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512], \"cudagraph_mode\": \"FULL_DECODE_ONLY\"}' \\\n  --additional-config '{\"enable_shared_expert_dp\": true, \"ascend_fusion_config\": {\"fusion_ops_gmmswigluquant\": false}}'\n```\n\n## Functional Verification\n\nOnce your server is started, you can query the model with input prompts:\n\n```shell\ncurl -H \"Accept: application/json\" \\\n    -H \"Content-type: application/json\" \\\n    -X POST \\\n    -d '{\n        \"model\": \"glm\", \n        \"messages\": [{ \n            \"role\": \"user\", \n            \"content\": \"The future of AI is\" \n        }], \n        \"stream\": false, \n        \"ignore_eos\": false, \n        \"temperature\": 0, \n        \"max_tokens\": 200 \n    }' http://<node0_ip>:<port>/v1/chat/completions\n```\n\n## Accuracy Evaluation\n\nHere are two accuracy evaluation methods.\n\n### Using AISBench\n\n1. Refer to [Using AISBench](../../developer_guide/evaluation/using_ais_bench.md) for details.\n\n2. After execution, you can get the result, here is the result of `GLM4.7` in `vllm-ascend:main` (after `vllm-ascend:0.14.0rc1`) for reference only.\n\n| dataset | version | metric | mode | vllm-api-general-chat | note |\n|----- | ----- | ----- | ----- | -----| ----- |\n| GPQA | - | accuracy | gen | 84.85 | 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) |\n| MATH500 | - | accuracy | gen | 98.8 | 1 Atlas 800 A3 (64G × 16) |\n\n### Using Language Model Evaluation Harness\n\nNot tested yet.\n\n## Performance\n\n### Using AISBench\n\nRefer to [Using AISBench for performance evaluation](../../developer_guide/evaluation/using_ais_bench.md#execute-performance-evaluation) for details.\n\n### Using vLLM Benchmark\n\nRun performance evaluation of `GLM-4.x` as an example.\n\nRefer to [vllm benchmark](https://docs.vllm.ai/en/latest/benchmarking/) for more details.\n\nThere are three `vllm bench` subcommands:\n\n- `latency`: Benchmark the latency of a single batch of requests.\n- `serve`: Benchmark the online serving throughput.\n- `throughput`: Benchmark offline inference throughput.\n\nTake the `serve` as an example. Run the code as follows.\n\n```shell\nvllm bench serve \\\n  --backend vllm \\\n  --dataset-name prefix_repetition \\\n  --prefix-repetition-prefix-len 22400 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-suffix-len 9600 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-output-len 1024 \\\n  --num-prompts 1 \\\n  --prefix-repetition-num-prefixes 1 \\\n  --ignore-eos \\\n  --model glm \\\n  --tokenizer Eco-Tech/GLM-4.7-W8A8-floatmtp \\\n  --seed 1000 \\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\n  --port 8000 \\\n  --endpoint /v1/completions \\\n  --max-concurrency 1 \\\n  --request-rate 1\n```\n\nAfter about several minutes, you can get the performance evaluation result.\n\n## Best Practices\n\nIn this chapter, we recommend best practices for three scenarios:\n\n- Long-context: For long sequences with low concurrency (≤ 4): set `dp1 tp16`; For long sequences with high concurrency (> 4): set `dp2 tp8`\n- Low-latency: For short sequences with low latency: we recommend setting `dp2 tp8`\n- High-throughput: For short sequences with high throughput: we also recommend setting `dp2 tp8`\n\n**Notice:**\n`max-model-len` and `max-num-seqs` need to be set according to the actual usage scenario. For other settings, please refer to the **[Deployment](#deployment)** chapter.\n\n## FAQ\n\n- **Q: Startup fails with HCCL port conflicts (address already bound). What should I do?**\n\n  A: Clean up old processes and restart: `pkill -f VLLM*`.\n\n- **Q: How to handle OOM or unstable startup?**\n\n  A: Reduce `--max-num-seqs` and `--max-model-len` first. If needed, reduce concurrency and load-testing pressure (e.g., `max-concurrency` / `num-prompts`).","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
